1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any extremal module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 47. The projective resolution has length at most 831 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the extremal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^47 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 831-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the extremal object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 779. The moduli space has dimension at most 9 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on extremal structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 779. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 9. QED.
